- **&lt;folder-name-lowercase-hyphens&gt;**: &lt;What it does. Use when the user asks for &lt;X&gt; or mentions &lt;Y&gt;.&gt; ([razorvision/clara-skills](https://github.com/razorvision/clara-skills/tree/HEAD/templates/minimal-skill))
- **&lt;folder-name-lowercase-hyphens&gt;**: &lt;What the skill does, in third person, one or two sentences. Then the trigger: Use when the user asks for &lt;X&gt;, mentions &lt;Y&gt;, or needs &lt;Z&gt;.&gt; ([razorvision/clara-skills](https://github.com/razorvision/clara-skills/tree/HEAD/templates/full-skill))
- **&lt;folder-name&gt;**: &lt;What it does, in one clause&gt;. Use when &lt;the phrases and situations a user actually brings, including ones that don't name the skill&gt;. Do NOT use for &lt;the nearest sibling cases, naming the skill that owns each&gt;. ([sadpandajoe/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/sadpandajoe/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/docs/templates) · ★1)
- **&lt;gerund-kebab-name&gt;**: Scaffold template — intentionally has a placeholder name that does NOT match its directory. The validator MUST skip `_`-prefixed dirs, so this file must not produce an error. ([praetorian-inc/public-workflows](https://github.com/praetorian-inc/public-workflows/tree/HEAD/_test-fixtures/skills-minimal/_template) · ★1)
- **&lt;hook-name&gt;**: &lt;What this skill does. Keep under 1024 chars.&gt; ([HugoLopes45/aiclair](https://github.com/HugoLopes45/aiclair/tree/HEAD/packages/hooks/_template) · ★3)
- **&lt;Human-readable role name&gt;**: One paragraph: what the role does and when it triggers. ([mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin](https://github.com/mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/src/skills/residential_multifamily/roles/_TEMPLATE) · ★52)
- **&lt;Human-readable workflow name&gt;**: One paragraph: what the workflow does and when it triggers. ([mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin](https://github.com/mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/src/skills/residential_multifamily/workflows/_TEMPLATE) · ★52)
- **&lt;job-slug&gt;**: &lt;Written for triggering: what job this performs, for which outcome, touching which systems, and the phrasings a user would actually say. No marketing copy.&gt; ([dstevenson1981/jtbd-agent-builder](https://github.com/dstevenson1981/jtbd-agent-builder/tree/HEAD/engine/agent-factory/templates/generated-skill))
- **&lt;kebab-case 技能名&gt;**: &lt;何时触发 + 做什么。写得具体、略"推"一点以利触发。不要写"用于 XX 任务"这种废描述。&gt; ([qihangzhang-272/agent-skill-library](https://github.com/qihangzhang-272/agent-skill-library/tree/HEAD/archive/legacy-plugin-layout/plugins/foundation/skills/skill-architecture/assets/skill-template) · ★2)
- **&lt;kebab-case, equals folder name&gt;**: Use when &lt;trigger in one sentence&gt;. &lt;What it produces, in one sentence.&gt; (2 sentences at most; "Use when" is required. Format adapted from addyosmani/agent-skills, MIT) ([mementomori-co/maker-mastery](https://github.com/mementomori-co/maker-mastery/tree/HEAD/templates/skill) · ★2)
- **&lt;kebab-case, ≤ 64 ký tự, ví dụ adding-module&gt;**: Kích hoạt khi &lt;từ khoá người dùng hay nói, tín hiệu cụ thể&gt; trong repo &lt;tên&gt;. &lt;Kết quả đầu ra, ngôi thứ ba, ≤ 450 ký tự; không tóm tắt các bước làm việc bên trong để tránh agent bỏ qua đọc thân file.&gt; ([huongmh14/writing-spec-for-ai](https://github.com/huongmh14/writing-spec-for-ai/tree/HEAD/templates/project-skill))
- **&lt;kebab-case-name-matching-folder&gt;**: One-line trigger that another agent will read to decide whether to invoke this skill. Lead with the verb, name the artifact. ([evolplus/talos](https://github.com/evolplus/talos/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★9)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name-matching-this-directory&gt;**: &lt;What it does, then when to use it. Add "Invoked by &lt;parent-skill&gt;; not a standalone entry point." when another skill is the only caller.&gt; ([uashogeschoolutrecht/hu-agent-framework](https://github.com/uashogeschoolutrecht/hu-agent-framework/tree/HEAD/plugins/hu-core/skills/make-skill/templates) · ★1)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;one-sentence trigger condition — when should this skill be loaded? Start with "Apply when ..." for consistency.&gt; ([sordi-ai/skill-everything](https://github.com/sordi-ai/skill-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★21)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;verb + object&gt;. Use when &lt;trigger phrase&gt;. ([santiment/skills](https://github.com/santiment/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★1)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;WHAT it does in one clause, then WHEN — trigger-dense: name the literal phrases that should invoke it ("run &lt;name&gt;", "review the &lt;artifact&gt;", "is this &lt;bar&gt;-compliant") AND the task-type trigger (the situation that should invoke it even when nobody says the name — e.g. "whenever you have built or changed X and are about to present it / reach an owner gate"). End with the NEGATIVE scope: "Do NOT use &lt;name&gt; for &lt;the neighboring lane&gt; — that is &lt;other skill&gt;'s job." This field is the router: everything the harness uses to decide whether to load the skill lives here, so spend words on triggers,… ([Aiden-Molyneaux/shipwright](https://github.com/Aiden-Molyneaux/shipwright/tree/HEAD/skills/_TEMPLATE))
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;This line decides whether the skill ever gets read — it matters more than the body. Start with "Use when..." and list concrete trigger situations: when to use it, phrases or contexts that should trigger it, and when NOT to use it.&gt; ([flll/grow-your-own-skills](https://github.com/flll/grow-your-own-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/_template))
- **&lt;kebab-case-nombre&gt;**: &lt;Una frase clara con los TRIGGERS — "Usar cuando ...". El agente decide cargarla por esta línea, así que sé concreto sobre cuándo aplica y qué cubre.&gt; ([hiramvazquez/iOSAppBaseline](https://github.com/hiramvazquez/iOSAppBaseline/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/_TEMPLATE-skill) · ★1)
- **&lt;kebab-case-skill-name&gt;**: &lt;一句话说明这个 Skill 做什么、什么时候触发。写得"主动"一些—— 涵盖用户可能的多种表述方式，包括直接调用和隐含需求的场景。 非空、≤1024 字符。这是触发机制的主要依据，注册脚本会读它写进 substage-skill-map.yaml。 所有"何时使用"的信息放这里，**不放正文**（不要写 "## When to use" 章节）。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★16)
- **&lt;kebab-case-skill-name&gt;**: &lt;One sentence — what the skill does + when to use it. Keep under ~280 chars. Include trigger phrases like "Use when the user asks for X, Y, or Z" so Claude knows to invoke.&gt; ([Euler-Software-Inc/EULER-skills](https://github.com/Euler-Software-Inc/EULER-skills/tree/HEAD/template))
- **&lt;kebab-case-skill-slug&gt;**: &lt;one sentence — the procedural / framing gap this skill closes for *this class of work* (e.g., "reviewing corporate authorization documents for a credit-facility closing across multi-entity, multi-jurisdiction guarantor groups"). Do not mention specific dollar amounts, percentages, named entities, verbatim quotes, or jurisdiction- / statute- / entity-type-specific citations that you only know to mention because you read *this* scenario's documents. The description should read like the title of a CLE module, not the abstract of a single matter.&gt; ([sunyifeisb-art/legalwork](https://github.com/sunyifeisb-art/legalwork/tree/HEAD/skills/awesome-legal-aiagent-skills/_template) · ★67)
- **&lt;kebab-case-slug&gt;**: &lt;What this skill does. Use this whenever the user mentions &lt;concrete contexts and trigger phrases&gt;, even if they don't explicitly ask for &lt;skill name&gt;.&gt; ([labs21-dev/agents-stack](https://github.com/labs21-dev/agents-stack/tree/HEAD/skill-creator/templates/skill-skeleton) · ★27)
- **&lt;kebab-case-slug&gt;**: &lt;What this skill does, in one sentence. Then the activation triggers: "Activates when &lt;task shapes, file types, or user phrasings that should fire this skill&gt;." The description is the ONLY signal harnesses use to auto-activate — write it for the router, not the reader.&gt; ([Neogenuity/harness-kit](https://github.com/Neogenuity/harness-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/harness-kit/skills/harness-kit/templates/docs/skills/_example))
- **&lt;kebab-case&gt;**: &lt;1 linha — o que esta skill ensina; usada por IAs para decidir relevância&gt; ([henriquessn1/fluxo-ideal-ia](https://github.com/henriquessn1/fluxo-ideal-ia/tree/HEAD/skills/_template))
- **&lt;kebab-name&gt;**: &lt;≤300 chars, third-person, behavioral, names what + when (trigger phrases)&gt; ([fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_Edition](https://github.com/fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_Edition/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/skill-creator/assets/skill-skeleton))
- **&lt;kebab-name&gt;**: &lt;one paragraph: what this playbook does AND when to reach for it — the description doubles as the when-to-use trigger.&gt; ([Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim](https://github.com/Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/_TEMPLATE))
- **&lt;kebab-name&gt;**: Use when &lt;situation and trigger words&gt; ([maximk777/opencode-config](https://github.com/maximk777/opencode-config/tree/HEAD/skills/agents-instrumentation/templates/skill))
- **&lt;layer&gt;-engineering-standards**: GENERATED ONCE PER LAYER/LANGUAGE of your chain by /adapt-to-stack, into the repo's own .claude/skills/ under a per-layer directory name (e.g. schema-standards, backend-standards, frontend-standards). The coding + review standard for &lt;LAYER/LANGUAGE&gt;. Loaded when writing or reviewing &lt;LANGUAGE&gt; code, or when running as the &lt;layer&gt;-specialist agent. Deliberately carries NO rules for other layers so it stays cheap to load. ([konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook](https://github.com/konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/engineering-standards) · ★3)
- **&lt;layer&gt;-write-core**: GENERATED ONCE PER LAYER of your chain by /adapt-to-stack, into the repo's own .claude/skills/ beside &lt;layer&gt;-standards. The short write-time rule set for &lt;LAYER/LANGUAGE&gt;: only the rules that are expensive to get wrong once the code compiles. Preloaded by &lt;layer&gt;-specialist and slice-&lt;layer&gt;-specialist through their skills: line, so it is in context for every edit. The full &lt;layer&gt;-standards is for review, not for writing. ([konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook](https://github.com/konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/write-core) · ★3)
- **&lt;lowercase-hyphenated-name&gt;**: &lt;what specialized capability this provides and when it should activate&gt; ([altertable-ai/skills](https://github.com/altertable-ai/skills/tree/HEAD/templates/SKILL_TEMPLATE) · ★7)
- **&lt;merge-train&gt;**: Use when the user asks to merge a batch of pull requests, or asks in what order to merge them. Has &lt;merge-order&gt; propose the order, then runs the one &lt;train command&gt; on the user's go-ahead. Never merges with --admin and never merges a pull request outside the train. ([mnaimfaizy/agent-kit](https://github.com/mnaimfaizy/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/examples/patterns/merge-train) · ★1)
- **&lt;name&gt;**: &lt;What the skill covers, in one sentence&gt;. Use this skill &lt;when: before or after which action, for which files or tasks&gt;. Use &lt;other-skill&gt; for &lt;the related task it does not cover&gt;. ([dougrocha/dotfiles](https://github.com/dougrocha/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/skill) · ★1)
- **&lt;name&gt;**: Guide any change to &lt;scope, in words&gt;. Use before touching &lt;paths&gt;, or when the user types /&lt;name&gt;. ([initialneil/coding-by-contract](https://github.com/initialneil/coding-by-contract/tree/HEAD/skills/coding-by-contract/templates) · ★1)

- **&lt;name&gt;**: &lt;What the skill does, in one sentence. Then when to use it, in the words people actually say, e.g. Use when the user asks to "…", "…" or "…".&gt; ([vignzpie/arsenal](https://github.com/vignzpie/arsenal/tree/HEAD/templates/skill))

- **&lt;name&gt;-level**: Audit &lt;what part of a project&gt; against a 5-level &lt;what the scale measures&gt; scale, propose options, then split the chosen work into sub-plans delegated to Haiku or Sonnet while Opus plans and reviews. - **&lt;plugin&gt;-config**: Configura &lt;plugin&gt; para el proyecto actual. Pregunta preferencias de forma interactiva y las guarda en &lt;datadir&gt;/config.json. - **&lt;plugin&gt;-context**: Punto de entrada único de &lt;plugin&gt;. Lee el contexto (git, archivos, mensaje del usuario, historial) y decide qué escenario aplicar. Activar para cualquier flujo del plugin; nunca invocar sub-skills privadas directamente. - **&lt;plugin&gt;-health**: Verifica que &lt;plugin&gt; está bien integrado: versión, skills, hooks, MCPs y estado del proyecto. Correr tras instalar/actualizar o para diagnosticar. - **&lt;plugin&gt;-propose**: Genera una propuesta de acción como archivo .md en proposals/ para revisión humana antes de ejecutar. El usuario aprueba, edita o descarta. - **&lt;plugin&gt;-vocab**: Guardián del vocabulario del plugin. Valida que todo término/estado/constante de dominio exista en config/vocabulary.json antes de usarlo. NUNCA inventar términos nuevos. ([WSmithDR/cli-plugin-template](https://github.com/WSmithDR/cli-plugin-template/tree/HEAD/features/vocabulary-guardian/files))
- **&lt;policy-name&gt;**: Policy - &lt;one line naming the rules, e.g. "API and security rules (auth on every route, rate limits, no secrets in logs)"&gt;. - **&lt;project&gt;-design**: &lt;Project&gt;'s design system — its colour roles, type, spacing and component recipes. - **&lt;provider-id&gt;**: &lt;What it does, one line&gt;. Trigger phrases: &lt;phrases&gt;. ([gabrielmoreira/agent-skills-mirror](https://github.com/gabrielmoreira/agent-skills-mirror/tree/HEAD/mirrors/repos/Anil-matcha%40awesome-muse-connectors/connectors/_template) · ★20 · 2 copies)

([mnaimfaizy/agent-kit](https://github.com/mnaimfaizy/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/examples/patterns/tiered-quality-gates) · ★1)

- **__FRONT_END_PLUGIN__-prototype**: Mock up a UI from a description, as a clickable page in the look of the team's design system, and show it to the person wherever this session can (a hosted page, the team's design tool, or a browser), then iterate on it from their feedback. - **__pr-review-loop**: Automatically run the Devin review-response loop for a target PR. Monitor the Devin session page directly → check reviews → triage into needs-action/no-action → push fixes → reply to each thread individually + resolve on Devin → monitor the next round (first check after 3 min → then every 30 sec) → monitor CI and handle errors — repeating until all reviews and CI are resolved, then report and await instructions. ([cilly-yllic/.llm](https://github.com/cilly-yllic/.llm/tree/HEAD/skills/en/__pr-review-loop))
